FROM (
    SELECT `u`.`Id`, `u`.`ActiveDirectorySid`, `u`.`CreatedAt`, `u`.`Email`, `u`.`FailedLoginAttempts`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`IsActive`, `u`.`LastName`, `u`.`LockedUntil`, `u`.`PasswordChangedAt`, `u`.`PasswordHash`, `u`.`UpdatedAt`, `u`.`Username`
    FROM `users` AS `u`
    WHERE (`u`.`Username` = @__username_0) AND `u`.`IsActive`
    LIMIT 1
) AS `t`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `u0`.`UserId`, `u0`.`RoleId`, `u0`.`AssignedAt`, `r`.`Id`, `r`.`Description`, `r`.`Name`, `r`.`Permissions`
    FROM `user_roles` AS `u0`
    INNER JOIN `roles` AS `r` ON `u0`.`RoleId` = `r`.`Id`
) AS `t0` ON `t`.`Id` = `t0`.`UserId`

`u`.`failed_login_attempts`, `u`.`first_name`, `u`.`is_active`, `u`.`last_name`, `u`.`locked_until`, `u`.`password_changed_at`, `u`.`password_hash`, `u`.`updated_at`, `u`.`username`, `u0`.`id`, `u0`.`password_hash`, `u0`.`updated_at`, `u0`.`username`, `t0`.`Id`, `t0`.`ApprovalLevel`, `t0`.`ApprovedAt`, `t0`.`ApproverId`, `t0`.`Comments`, `t0`.`CreatedAt`, `t0`.`InvoiceId`, `t0`.`RuleId`, `t0`.`Status`, `t0`.`StepNumber`, `t0`.`id0`, `t0`.`active_directory_sid`, `t0`.`created_at`, `t0`.`email`, `t0`.`failed_login_attempts`, `t0`.`first_name`, `t0`.`is_active`, `t0`.`last_name`, `t0`.`locked_until`, `t0`.`password_changed_at`, `t0`.`password_hash`, `t0`.`updated_at`, `t0`.`username`
FROM (
    SELECT `i`.`Id`, `i`.`ApprovalLevel`, `i`.`AutoApproved`, `i`.`CostCenterId`, `i`.`CreatedAt`, `i`.`CreatedBy`, `i`.`Currency`, `i`.`Description`, `i`.`DueDate`, `i`.`InternalNotes`, `i`.`InvoiceDate`, `i`.`InvoiceNumber`, `i`.`NetAmount`, `i`.`OriginalFilename`, `i`.`PdfFilePath`, `i`.`PdfFileSize`, `i`.`ProcessedBy`, `i`.`ProjectId`, `i`.`PurchaseOrderId`, `i`.`ReceivedDate`, `i`.`RequiresApproval`, `i`.`Status`, `i`.`SupplierId`, `i`.`TaxAmount`, `i`.`TotalAmount`, `i`.`UpdatedAt`
    FROM `invoices` AS `i`
    ORDER BY `i`.`DueDate`
    LIMIT @__p_1 OFFSET @__p_0
) AS `t`
INNER JOIN `suppliers` AS `s` ON `t`.`SupplierId` = `s`.`Id`
LEFT JOIN `cost_centers` AS `c` ON `t`.`CostCenterId` = `c`.`Id`
LEFT JOIN `projects` AS `p` ON `t`.`ProjectId` = `p`.`Id`
LEFT JOIN `users` AS `u` ON `t`.`CreatedBy` = `u`.`id`
LEFT JOIN `users` AS `u0` ON `t`.`ProcessedBy` = `u0`.`id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `a`.`Id`, `a`.`ApprovalLevel`, `a`.`ApprovedAt`, `a`.`ApproverId`, `a`.`Comments`, `a`.`CreatedAt`, `a`.`InvoiceId`, `a`.`RuleId`, `a`.`Status`, `a`.`StepNumber`, `u1`.`id` AS `id0`, `u1`.`active_directory_sid`, `u1`.`created_at`, `u1`.`email`, `u1`.`failed_login_attempts`, `u1`.`first_name`, `u1`.`is_active`, `u1`.`last_name`, `u1`.`locked_until`, `u1`.`password_changed_at`, `u1`.`password_hash`, `u1`.`updated_at`, `u1`.`username`
    FROM `approval_workflows` AS `a`
    INNER JOIN `users` AS `u1` ON `a`.`ApproverId` = `u1`.`id`
) AS `t0` ON `t`.`Id` = `t0`.`InvoiceId`
ORDER BY `t`.`DueDate`, `t`.`Id`, `s`.`Id`, `c`.`Id`, `p`.`Id`, `u`.`id`, `u0`.`id`, `t0`.`Id`

SELECT `t`.`id`, `t`.`active_directory_sid`, `t`.`created_at`, `t`.`email`, `t`.`failed_login_attempts`, `t`.`first_name`, `t`.`is_active`, `t`.`last_name`, `t`.`locked_until`, `t`.`password_changed_at`, `t`.`password_hash`, `t`.`updated_at`, `t`.`username`, `t0`.`user_id`, `t0`.`role_id`, `t0`.`assigned_at`, `t0`.`id`, `t0`.`description`, `t0`.`name`, `t0`.`permissions`
FROM (
    SELECT `u`.`id`, `u`.`active_directory_sid`, `u`.`created_at`, `u`.`email`, `u`.`failed_login_attempts`, `u`.`first_name`, `u`.`is_active`, `u`.`last_name`, `u`.`locked_until`, `u`.`password_changed_at`, `u`.`password_hash`, `u`.`updated_at`, `u`.`username`
    FROM `users` AS `u`
    WHERE (`u`.`username` = @__username_0) AND `u`.`is_active`
    LIMIT 1
) AS `t`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `u0`.`user_id`, `u0`.`role_id`, `u0`.`assigned_at`, `r`.`id`, `r`.`description`, `r`.`name`, `r`.`permissions`
    FROM `user_roles` AS `u0`
    INNER JOIN `roles` AS `r` ON `u0`.`role_id` = `r`.`id`
